# Listed differences on the latest master

[English](differences-on-master.md) / [日本語](differences-on-master.ja.md)

The [list of differences](https://github.com/kurochan001/kirimasim-tests/blob/68f5ecc7507d6157f7944b48aa50c61fcc6fa2e1/docs/simulator-differences.md) records where the releases Icarus Verilog 13.0 and Verilator 5.052 differ from the standard, with the tests that show it. This page gives, for each test it names, the result of the latest nightly run of the same simulator, which the nightly workflow builds from its latest `master`. A test that matches there may have been fixed upstream: a person checks that before the item in the list changes. `tools/differences_status.py` writes this page and changes nothing in the list.

| Simulator | Version of the run | Run (UTC) | Suite | Named tests | Still differ | Now match | Not run |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Icarus Verilog | Icarus Verilog version 14.0 (devel) (s20260301-964-g3c970ad8) | 2026-11-12 08:32 | [`68f5ecc`](https://github.com/kurochan001/kirimasim-tests/commit/68f5ecc7507d6157f7944b48aa50c61fcc6fa2e1) | 236 | 203 | 33 | 0 |
| Verilator | Verilator 5.055 devel rev v5.054-219-g12ce8608 | 2026-11-12 08:32 | [`68f5ecc`](https://github.com/kurochan001/kirimasim-tests/commit/68f5ecc7507d6157f7944b48aa50c61fcc6fa2e1) | 487 | 427 | 60 | 0 |

Named tests counts each test once per simulator. Patterns and the items under "To check in the list" are not in these counts.
